{"forms":[{"form":"circumlocutions","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A roundabout or indirect way of speaking; thus"],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A roundabout or indirect way of speaking; thus:","Unnecessary use of extra words to express an idea, such as a pleonastic phrase (sometimes driven by an attempt at emphatic clarity) or a wordy substitution (the latter driven by euphemistic intent, pedagogic intent, or sometimes loquaciousness alone)."],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"A technical word, such as hyperkalemia or hypoallergenic, can be glossed for general audiences with a circumlocution, such as \"high potassium level\" or \"less likely to cause allergies\" (respectively).","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A roundabout or indirect way of speaking; thus:","Necessary use of a phrase to circumvent either a vocabulary fault (of speaker or listener) or a lexical gap, either monolingually or in translation."],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"glosses":["An instance of such usage; a roundabout expression, whether an inadvisable one or a necessary one."],"tags":["countable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˌsɜːkəmləˈkjuːʃən/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˌsɝkəmləˈkjuʃən/","tags":["General-American"]}],"synonyms":[{"word":"ambages"},{"word":"periphrasis"}],"upstream_line":291472,"upstream_offset":1455345890,"upstream_record_sha256":"1bf5a8b2eb2a1c9fca02e914a5cc57ef75f2f0cc329f06acc488db67211369dc","word":"circumlocution"}

{"forms":[{"form":"handicaps","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Age is often a handicap.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Something that prevents, hampers, or hinders."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The older boy won, even though his opponent had been granted a handicap of five meters.","type":"example"},{"text":"A handicap in chess often involves removal of the queen's rook.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An allowance of a certain amount of time or distance in starting, granted in a race (or other contest of skill) to the competitor possessing disadvantages; or an additional weight or other hindrance imposed upon the one possessing advantages, in order to equalize, as much as possible, the chances of success."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["hobbies","lifestyle","sports"]},{"glosses":["An individual's physical or mental disadvantage or disability."],"qualifier":"sometimes considered offensive","tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A race or similar contest in which there is an allowance of time, distance, weight, or other advantage, to equalize the chances of the competitors."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["An old card game, similar to lanterloo."],"tags":["obsolete","uncountable"],"topics":["card-games","games"]},{"glosses":["Synonym of hand-in-cap (“old English trading game”)."],"synonyms":[{"tags":["synonym","synonym-of"],"word":"hand-in-cap"}],"tags":["obsolete","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈhændikæp/"}],"upstream_line":24759,"upstream_offset":169220864,"upstream_record_sha256":"a815a8dd08b8bf54d2439314232090e24e18e49f2c7583842e4d15947fe21936","word":"handicap"}

{"forms":[{"form":"monotone functions","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"monotonic function","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Where defined, the first derivative of a monotone function never changes sign, although it may be zero.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A function f : X→R (where X is a subset of R, possibly a discrete set) that either never decreases or never increases as its independent variable increases; that is, either x ≤ y implies f(x) ≤ f(y) or x ≤ y implies f(y) ≤ f(x)."],"topics":["calculus","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A function f : X→Y (where X and Y are posets with partial order \"≤\") with either: (1) the property that x ≤ y implies f(x) ≤ f(y), or (2) the property that x ≤ y implies f(y) ≤ f(x)."],"topics":["mathematical-analysis","mathematics","order-theory","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A Boolean function with the property that switching any one input variable from 0 to 1 results either in no change in output or a change from 0 to 1."],"qualifier":"Boolean algebra"}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"function that either never decreases or never increases","word":"monotonic function"}],"upstream_line":1145478,"upstream_offset":4083381851,"upstream_record_sha256":"e5681cd7645f4cd967f589113bbf671b85eaeb3604e8c0778454777fb44be361","word":"monotone function"}
